Detailed optimizer design:

We will implement two local optimizers:
A. Smoothed max logistics with Adam natural gradient and line search, with active-set sharpening.
B. Top-active quadratic/proximal? Could be similar.

We'll also implement a "constraint perturb" method: Given p, identify lags where c is high. We can directly adjust p to reduce each high lag by scaling involved indices. For a single lag k, the subgradient is 2p_{k-i}. The natural gradient update with λ over top lags is multiplicative. We can use multiple λ distributions:
- softmax top with temperature τ.
- uniform over top K lags.
- weights proportional to (c_k - mean)^+.
Then apply step.

Line search:
Given w current, p. Compute w_new = w - lr * normalized_direction (natural gradient). Need choose lr. We can compute obj for lr candidates: [1,0.5,2,0.25,4] scaled. Since objective scale invariant, use FFT. Accept best. If none better, reduce lr. For speed, each evaluation FFT O(n log n) ~milliseconds; 5 per step okay. We can limit steps per second.

However natural gradient direction may be poor if using softmax over all 9600 lags. Computing λ each step cheap. Computing gradient via FFT O(n log n). For n=5000, maybe 5000*log ~65k; many steps possible (minutes => 100k steps? but Python overhead). We can do ~10k steps maybe.

We can also use a cheaper Euclidean gradient using top lags direct? If top K small (e.g., 128), g_i = 2∑_{k∈K, weight} p_{k-i}. Direct O(nK)=640k per step, maybe okay but FFT better.

Important: Score includes normalization sum=1. In update, if we rescale p to sum1 after log step, we need adjust gradient w.r.t log variables. We'll use natural gradient direction d_i = p_i*(grad_i - p·grad) (if p normalized). Or simply w_i -= lr*grad_i then rescale p; this corresponds to not subtracting mean, but log softmax normalization implicitly handles? If w_i -= lr*grad_i, p=exp(w)/sum. Derivative of objective wrt w includes subtraction mean. If we don't subtract, update includes a common shift (which doesn't change p) plus -mean grad? Actually exp(w - lr grad)/S: common term? Since grad varies, cannot separate. The difference from subtracting mean is adding c (constant) to all w, which doesn't change p. Wait if we update w_i -= lr grad_i, then relative to w_i' = w_i - lr(grad_i - mean_grad), the difference is -lr mean_grad constant, which is irrelevant after softmax. So we don't need subtract mean for p, only for direction scale. Adam per-coordinate might be affected by constant offset? Adam uses gradient values; constant offset changes m/v equally but per-coordinate? The update mhat - eta? If add constant to all grad, each coordinate raw grad changes, but Adam normalization maybe not invariant. Better subtract mean to remove common mode and improve. We'll compute grad_w = p*(grad - dot(p,grad)).

But we can also use Euclidean gradient projected: p_new = p - η grad, project positive/normalize. Might be okay. Natural log better for positivity.

Let's verify gradient of c_k: c_k from np.convolve(a,a). For normalized sum=1. For k, ∂c_k/∂p_i = 2 p_{k-i} if 0≤k-i<n, else 0. Is that true for k outside 0..2n-2? yes zero. We can compute g_i=2∑_k λ_k p_{k-i}. This is cross-correlation between p (indexed i) and λ (lag difference). Specifically g_i/2 = ∑_{j=0}^{n-1} p_j λ_{i+j} with i+j<2n-1. If we use λ over m=2n-1 padded to N, and p length n. The FFT formula T1_i = ifft(conj(FFT(p,N)) * FFT(λ,N)) at i? Earlier test with n=2: λ length3, p [a,b], T1_0 = ifft(conj(F)*L)_0 = aλ0+bλ1 correct. So g_i=2*T1_i. Great.

We can implement:
```
def smooth_gradient(p, lam, N):
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    L = np.fft.rfft(lam, N)  # lam length m=2n-1
    h = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(P) * L, N)
    g = 2.0 * h[:n]
```
Need no phase. Let's test with n=2, P rfft? For N≥3. DFT linear correlations with no wrap. Should work. However if p,λ real, P*conj? conj(P)*L. Yes.

Adam step:
```
def adam_update(w, d, m, v, t, lr):
   t +=1
   b1=0.9, b2=0.999
   m = b1*m + (1-b1)*d
   v = b2*v + (1-b2)*d*d
   mhat = m/(1-b1**t)
   vhat = v/(1-b2**t)
   step = mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+eps)
   step = clip(step, -10,10)
   w_new = w - lr*step
   p_new = softmax(w_new)
   return p_new, w_new, m,v,t
```

How to solve LP efficiently? Variables b_i≥0 (n), constraints (f*b)_k ≤ M (m~2n), objective ∑b. Dual:
Primal: max 1^T b
 s.t. C b ≤ M 1, b≥0. (No sum constraint)
Dual: min M 1^T λ
 s.t. C^T λ ≥ 1, λ≥0.
Value S* = min M ∑λ_i subject (Hλ f)_i ≥1. Let μ_i = M λ_i; then minimize ∑μ_i subject (Hμ f)_i ≥ M, μ≥0. The constraints (Hμ f)_i = ∑_k μ_k f_{k-i}. The dual is linear. Let's design an improved `subproblem_step_block(f, b, w_b, m_b, v_b, t_b, steps, tau_factor, lr)`:
- Precompute F=rfft(f,N).
- For each of steps:
   - B=rfft(b,N); c=irfft(F*B,N)[:m]
   - mx=max(c); tau=mx/active K (e.g., 32) or adapt.
   - lambda=softmax(c/tau)
   - L=rfft(lambda,N); h=irfft(np.conj(F)*L,N)[:n]
   - d = b*(h - dot(b,h))
   - Adam update with lr_b; line search maybe optional. Since subproblem convex, accept if sub_obj decreases? We can maintain sub_score=mx (or smooth). Could just update blindly with Adam and decreasing lr. But b should improve sub problem; if not, line search.
- After block, return b (normalized), maybe sub_max.
Then blend f with b using multiple α; accept if main score improves.

But 
If b is the minimizer of max(f*b), its self conv may be bad. But blending small α helps. If f is near optimal, b≈f and no improvement; gradient self needed.

We can also perform **self-subproblem**: For fixed f, solve same for h? The self-gradient step is essentially b optimized for f equal p? We can just do self natural gradient of max f*f. That is also convex? min? Self objective not convex. But local.

Maybe the best algorithm is to alternate:
1. Optimize b against f (convex subproblem) for some steps.
2. Blend f with b.
3. Optimize f directly (self gradient) for some steps.
4. Repeat.
This is same as previous but with more robust subproblem and line search.

Additional improvement: Instead of blending with b in Euclidean space, use **geometric interpolation in log space**: p = softmax((1-α)log f + α log b). This preserves positivity and maybe better for sparse patterns. Evaluate both. Also try p = normalize((1-α)f+α b) and p = normalize(f^{1-α} b^{α}).

We can find best α by grid (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.33,0.5,0.75,1) with FFT score. If best > current, accept and reset self state.

We can also use the b subproblem not to replace f but as a "mirror" dual: The subproblem optimum value v = min_b max f*b. If v close to max f*f, f near optimal; if far, blend helps. We can estimate v by sub_score at b. Use adaptive blending density.

Potential numerical issue: b values can become extremely peaked; softmax underflow. Use w_b log state.

Let's design a time-efficient optimizer:

There will be a global best exact score. For each initial candidate, we run `optimize_f` with budget.

Pseudo for `optimize_f(f, budget, seed)`:
```
initialize p=f normalized, n, N.
w_f, m_f,v_f,t_f; lr_f=0.05
b=p; w_b, m_b,v_b,t_b; lr_b=0.1
A = rfft(p); c=...; score=...
best=...
outer=0
stall=0
while time<deadline:
  outer+=1
  # compute c/score
  A, c, score = fft_score_A_c(p,N)
  if score < best: update top/exact
  # Subproblem block for b against p
  steps_b = 10 if small else 20, maybe up to 50 if improving score
  sub_score = subproblem_block(p, b, state_b, steps_b, N, F=rfft(p))
  # Try blends
  best_candidate = None
  for alpha in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.33,0.5,0.8,1.0]:
      cand = blend(p,b,alpha)
      sc=fft_score_only(cand,N)
      if sc < score - tol: choose best
      also if sc < best: update
  if best_candidate improved:
      p=cand; reset w/m/v? Maybe reuse log
      A=...
      # update b? keep b or reset to p after large alpha
      if alpha>0.9: b=p
      reset self state (m_f) perhaps
      stall=0
      increase lr_b if sub helped
  else:
      # Direct self gradient block
      steps_self = 5
      self_step_block(p, state_f, N, ... ) with line search
      if improved: stall=0
      else:
         stall +=1
         if stall > threshold:
             reset to best + small perturbation; or random restart
  # Periodic exact validation for best
```

However doing `fft_score_A_c(p)` every outer and then subproblem block recomputes conv p*b many times. We can optimize.

Subproblem block details:
We want to keep F=rfft(p) fixed. For each step:
- B=rfft(b,N); cross=irfft(F*B,N)[:m]; mxc=max(cross); if not finite break.
- λ: We can choose top K uniform rather than softmax to stabilize. Let's use both? For each block, pick λ_topK with K = active_count = max(16, int(0.05*m))? But too many. Use softmax with tau = mxc / K, K = 64 (or adaptive 16-256). `lam = softmax(cross / tau)` stable. To prevent tiny, add uniform 1e-3? maybe not.
- L=rfft(lam,N); h=irfft(conj(F)*L,N)[:n]
- d=b*(h - dot(b,h))
- Adam update with lr_b: we can line search on sub_score (mxc for b? Use smooth score = logsumexp(cross/tau)*tau or mxc). Since subproblem convex, we can try multiplier of Adam step [1,0.5,2] and accept if sub smooth decreases. But mxc may not decrease monotonically. Use smooth objective φ=tau*logsumexp(cross/tau) for line search; then check exact max at end.
- State update.
Number of steps: maybe 20-50. But if p changes, b may need many. We can cache b and only run sub block every loop.

Blending: If b is minimizer, the ideal α? For small α, p_new. The score of blend can be evaluated. We could also do a line search in α using golden if cost okay (7 evaluations). 7 FFT scores ~ cheap. Do it whenever sub block cheap.

- Use F/rfft p, c. λ Grandma softmax(c/tau). h=irfft(conj(F)*L)[:n]; grad=2*h (or h? For self, derivative c_k wrt p_i =2p_{k-i}; if λ fixed, gradient wrt p =2∑λ_k p_{k-i}. This is 2*T1 with p itself. - Adam step with lr_self; line search on main score. Accept if improves current p. Use several steps.
- However self gradient may be slow; we can also do **active set exact subgradient**: use uniform top K. We can choose K=64. Good.

Let's design `optimize_one` in detail.

Initialization of a sequence `a` normalized sum1. n fixed.
Set N=next_fast_len(2*n-1).
Compute A,c,score.
Best sequence `best_f` copy, best_score.
top list max 12.
Exact best maybe score initial exact if n<=MAX_EXACT and time.

States:
- For f direct: `w_f=log_weights(f); m_f=zeros; v_f=zeros; t_f=0; lr_self=0.02?`
- For b subproblem: `b=f.copy(); w_b=log_weights(b); m_b=zeros; v_b=zeros; t_b=0; lr_b=0.05`.

**softmax over cross values**:
```
def softmax_values(x, temperature):
    # x array length L, temperature >0; returns weights sum1.
    y = x / temperature
    y = y - max(y)
    # if range too large, exp underflow okay.
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = e.sum()
    if s==0: uniform? 
    return e/s
```
For b update with h (minimize), we need softmax(-h/τ). Use same with -h.

**Adam step details**:
```
def adam_log_step(w, d, m, v, t, lr, clip=10):
    t += 1
    b1=0.9; b2=0.999
    m = m*b1 + (1-b1)*d
    v = v*b2 + (1-b2)*d*d
    mhat = m/(1-b1**t)
    vhat = v/(1-b2**t)
    step = mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-12)
    step = np.clip(step, -clip, clip)
    w_new = w - lr*step
    # clean
    w_new = np.where(np.isfinite(w_new), w_new, 0.0)
    w_new -= w_new.max()
    p = softmax_w(w_new)
    return p, w_new, m, v, t
```
But if line search tries multiple lrs from same m/v, we need not recompute m/v each candidate. We'll compute `m_new,v_new` and `step` once; then candidate w = w - mult*lr_base*step. If reject, we might still update m/v? Let's keep old m/v to maintain state consistent with accepted steps. For rejected, not update. But then if direction persists, next step recomputes m from old +d. Okay.

However if we reject due to no improvement but d was valid, not updating may cause repeated same. We can reduce lr and next time maybe accept. Good.

  d=b*(h-dot(b,h))
  # normalize d to avoid huge
  norm = sqrt(mean(d*d)) or max
  d_norm = d/norm
  # base lr maybe 0.1

      if smooth_try < smooth_cur - 1e-12 or mult==first: accept best
  if best_accept:
      return p_new, w_new, m_try, v_try, t+1, best_score
  else:
      return old (m/v unchanged), increase? But we need update m/v? We can update with direction but no accept? This would change future step. We'll not.

```
def natural_step(p,w,grad,n):
   d = p*(grad - dot(p,grad))
   scale = np.sqrt(np.mean(d*d))
   if scale>0: d = d/scale
   return d
```

Let's implement direct gradient function:
```
def top_k_shift_sum(seq, idx, out=None):
    # seq length n, idx length m <=2n-1 nonnegative ints.
    # compute g_i = sum_k seq[k-i] with 0<=k-i<n, average.
    g = zeros(n)
    for k in idx:
        # i ranges max(0, k-(n-1)) to min(n-1, k)
        i_start = max(0, k - (n-1))
        i_end = min(n-1, k)
        # g[i_start:i_end+1] += seq[k - i_end : k - i_start + 1]? Need seq index j=k-i ranges from k-i_start down to k-i_end.
        j_start = k - i_end
        j_end = k - i_start
        g[i_start:i_end+1] += seq[j_start:j_end+1][::-1]
    return g / len(idx)
```
For self, seq=p. For cross, seq=f. This loops K. For K=64,n=5000: 64 slices, okay. Need allocate/reuse to avoid overhead? We can `g.fill(0)` and loop. For speed, use `np.add`? `g[start:end] += seq[jstart:jend][::-1]` creates slice views reverse, okay. For K maybe 128.

This direct gradient is exact for λ uniform over top K. The smoothed softmax λ may be better, but uniform top K approximates active set and is cheaper. We can also use weighted top K with c values without FFT? If weights not all equal but only top K, still direct loop with weights. We can choose K adaptive, and weights proportional to maybe `c_k` or `(c_k - threshold)`. For K=64, direct fine. We can use `idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]` maybe; if K>=m use all. But argpartition O(m) C fast. Sorting not necessary. Weighted: `wts = c[idx]; wts /= sum` (or powers maybe). Direct loop with weights. This avoids full softmax λ and two FFTs per gradient. Huge improvement! We can run many more steps.

Implementation of direct weighted sum:

def add_shifted(seq, k, weight, g):
    n=len(seq)
    i0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
    i1 = min(n - 1, k)
    if i0 <= i1:
        j0 = k - i1
        j1 = k - i0
        g[i0:i1+1] += weight * seq[j0:j1+1][::-1]
```

Now, subproblem step with direct top K:
```
def cross_constrained_update(f,F,b,w_b,s_b,lr,K,N):
    B = rfft(b,N); cross=irfft(F*B,N)[:m]; if min<0 clip, mx=max
    idx = top_lags(cross,K)
    weights = cross[idx]; weights/=weights.sum()
    h = direct_weighted_shift_sum(f, idx, weights)
    d = b*(h - dot(b,h))
    # Adam normalized
    ... update b
    return b,w_b,s_b,sub_mx
```
Note: The subproblem objective max(f*b). Using top K weighted by cross values approximates gradient of smooth max. Since f,b nonnegative, h nonnegative. Good.

Resampling function:
```
def resample_sequence(seq, target_n):
  a=normalize(seq); n0=len(a); if n0==target return a
  x0=np.linspace(0,1,n0); x1=np.linspace(0,1,target_n)
  # use cumulative interpolation to preserve mass: new values = np.interp(x1, x0, a) maybe; then positive normalize
  b=np.interp(x1, x0, a); b=np.maximum(b,0); b/=b.sum(); return b
```
This linearly interpolates probabilities. For downsample, maybe bin means better: `blocks = reshape`; but interpolation okay.

Let's implement carefully:
```
def weighted_shift_sum(seq, k_arr, w_arr):
    n=seq.size
    g=np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for kk, wt in zip(k_arr, w_arr):
        k=int(kk)
        i0 = 0 if k >= n-1 else k-(n-1)
        i1 = n-1 if k >= n-1 else k
        if i0 <= i1:
            j0 = k - i1
            j1 = k - i0
            # seq[j0:j1+1] reversed corresponds to i0..i1
            g[i0:i1+1] += wt * seq[j0:j1+1][::-1]
    return g
```
If weights sum not 1, doesn't matter but d changes; we normalize.

We can avoid converting k to int each time by `k_arr = k_arr.astype(np.intp)`. `argpartition` returns int64.

Now, `fft_score_A_c` should compute stable. For p maybe sum not exactly1; but we maintain sum1. Score = 2n max(c). If c negative tiny, clip. If max huge? finite.

```
def fft_score_full(p,N):
    n=len(p)
    A=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    c=np.where(c>0,c,0)
    mx=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0
    score=2*n*mx
    return A,c,mx,score
```
Candidate score only: same without A maybe.

def subproblem_block(p, F, b, w_b, st_b, lr, K, N, rng):
   n=len(p); m=2*n-1
   sub_mx_list=[]
   for step in range(steps) and time<...:
       B=np.fft.rfft(b,N)
       cross=np.fft.irfft(F*B,N)[:m]
       cross=np.maximum(cross,0)
       mx=float(np.max(cross)) if cross.size else 0
       if not finite: break
       idx = choose_idx(cross,K,rng)
       wts = cross[idx]; wts/=wts.sum()
       h = weighted_shift_sum(p, idx, wts)
       d = b*(h - np.dot(b,h))
       # normalize and adam
       p_new, w_b_new, m,v,t = rms_adam_step(w_b,d,st_b,lr)
       b=p_new; w_b=w_b_new; st_b=...
   # compute final sub mx with new b (optional)
   B=... cross ... final_mx
   return b,w_b,st_b, final_mx
```
But this computes final cross extra; maybe next outer does. We can use last mx before update as approximate. For blend decision, need final b. We can compute final cross at end (one extra). Fine.

`rms_adam_step` top-level:
```
def rms_adam_step(w, d, m, v, t, lr, beta1=0.9,beta2=0.999, eps=1e-12, clip=8):
    t += 1
    m = beta1*m + (1-beta1)*d
    v = beta2*v + (1-beta2)*d*d
    mhat = m/(1-beta1**t)
    vhat = v/(1-beta2**t)
    step = mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+eps)
    step = np.where(np.isfinite(step), step, 0.0)
    # RMS normalize to unit average (excluding zeros?)
    sd = np.sqrt(np.mean(step*step))
    if sd > 0: step = step/sd
    step = np.clip(step, -clip, clip)
    w_new = w - lr*step
    w_new = np.where(np.isfinite(w_new), w_new, 0.0)
    w_new -= float(np.max(w_new))
    p = softmax_w(w_new)
    return p,w_new,m,v,t
```
Because step RMS=1, lr is max log change. lr_b 0.05-0.2. For p uniform, moving one coordinate by ±0.1 changes mass ~10%, reasonable.

We might want to use separate `clip` 0.5? If step RMS=1, individual can be larger due to skew; clip 4.

For subproblem, d = b*(h - mean). If b has many tiny, d small; RMS normalization handles. For self, d = p*(2h - mean(2h)).

Learning rate schedule: 
```
lr_b = 0.08; lr_self=0.05
```
If we use RMS step, maybe lr 0.05 good.

      sc = fft_score_only(cand,N)
      if sc < best_sc: store
      # geometric if a in (0.5,?)
      if a in [0.33,0.66] ...
   return best
```

Implement `propose_candidate`:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start_total=time.monotonic(); deadline=start_total + min(float(budget_s),2395)
    total = max(5.0, deadline-time)
    # get parent
    parent = load_initial_sequence()
    if parent is None: parent = power_candidate(4096)

       print
    # Also maybe take top candidate and extra optimize again?

"""
Searches nonnegative sequences minimizing ... using active-set natural gradient
and convex cross-convolution subproblem updates.

`normalize_nonneg`:
```
def normalize_nonneg(a):
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size==0: arr=np.ones(1)
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr),arr,0.0)
    arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
    s=float(arr.sum())
    if not finite or s<=0: arr=np.ones(max(1, arr.size)); s=arr.size
    return arr/s
```

`softmax_w` stable:
```
def softmax_w(w):
    w=np.asarray(w,dtype=np.float64)
    if w.size==0: return np.ones(1)
    w=np.where(np.isfinite(w),w,0.0)
    mx=float(np.max(w))
    e=np.exp(w-mx)
    s=float(e.sum())
    if not finite or s<=0: return np.ones(w.size)/w.size
    return e/s
```
`log_weights`:
```
def log_weights(p):
   p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel()
   p=np.maximum(p,1e-300)
   w=np.log(p)
   w=np.where(np.isfinite(w),w,0.0)
   w-=float(np.max(w))
   return w
```
But if p has zeros, log 1e-300 ~ -690, okay. If p underflow zero but sum1, support small; log weights very negative. Softmax recovers small positives. Good.

`next_fast_len`:
```
def next_fast_len(m):
    if m<=1: return 1
    p=1
    while p<m: p<<=1
    return p
```

`fft_score_full`:
```
def fft_score_full(a,N):
   n=len(a); m=2*n-1
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)
   c=c[:m]
   c=np.where(c>0,c,0.0)
   if c.size:
     mx=float(np.max(c))
   else: mx=0.0
   if not np.isfinite(mx): return A,c,0,INF
   sc=float(2.0*n*mx)
   return A,c,mx,sc
```
`fft_score_only`:
```
def fft_score_only(a,N):
   n=len(a); A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); c=irfft(A*A,N)[:2*n-1]; ... return sc
```
Could reuse full to avoid duplicate; but only need score. Use full if need A.

def exact_score_array(a):

`choose_active_indices`:
```
def choose_active_indices(c, K, rng=None):
    m=c.size
    if K>=m: return np.arange(m)
    pool=int(min(m, max(2*K, K+8)))
    # if pool>m pool=m
    # argpartition largest pool
    if pool == m:
       pool_idx=np.arange(m)
    else:
       pool_idx=np.argpartition(c, -pool)[-pool:]
    if K>=pool:
       return pool_idx
    if rng is None:
       return pool_idx[:K]
    return rng.choice(pool_idx, size=K, replace=False)
```
Note `np.argpartition(c, -pool)` requires kth < len; if pool=0? K>0. Good.

`active_weights`:
```
def make_weights_from_c(c, idx, sharp=1.0):
    vals=c[idx].astype(float)
    vals=np.where(np.isfinite(vals), vals, 0.0)
    vals=np.maximum(vals,0.0)
    if len(vals)==0: return vals
    if sharp!=1.0: vals=np.power(vals+1e-300, sharp)
    s=float(vals.sum())
    if s<=0 or not finite: return np.ones(len(vals))/len(vals)
    return vals/s
```

`weighted_shift_sum` as above. To avoid allocating g each time? We allocate. Could pass out buffer to reuse. Implement with optional `out`:
```
def weighted_shift_sum(seq, k_arr, w_arr, out=None):
   n=seq.size
   if out is None: g=np.zeros(n)
   else: g=out; g.fill(0.0)
   for kk, wt in zip(k_arr, w_arr):
      if wt==0: continue
      k=int(kk)
      if k<0 or k>=2*n-1: continue
      i0=0 if k>=n-1 else k-(n-1)
      i1=n-1 if k>=n-1 else k
      if i0<=i1:
         j0=k-i1; j1=k-i0
         g[i0:i1+1] += wt * seq[j0:j1+1][::-1]
   return g
```
Note for k maybe np.int64, `int(k)`.

`rms_adam_step` top-level. We'll use state arrays and t integer. Need pass `lr`. Step normalized. If `step` all zero (d zero), return same p? If sd=0, w_new=w, p=softmax_w(w). But softmax may shift due to centering; okay. We can if sd==0 return p,w,m,v,t? But m/v updated. p = normalize_nonneg? Use current p? To avoid recomputing if no change, maybe return p unchanged. But function doesn't know p. We can just softmax.

```
def rms_adam_step(w, d, m, v, t, lr, beta1=0.9,beta2=0.999,eps=1e-12,clip=5.0):
    t += 1
    d=np.where(np.isfinite(d),d,0.0)
    m = beta1*m + (1-beta1)*d
    v = beta2*v + (1-beta2)*d*d
    mhat=m/(1-beta1**t)
    vhat=v/(1-beta2**t)
    step=mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+eps)
    step=np.where(np.isfinite(step),step,0.0)
    sd=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(step*step)))
    if sd>EPS:
       step=step/sd
       step=np.clip(step,-clip,clip)
    w_new=w - lr*step
    w_new=np.where(np.isfinite(w_new), w_new, 0.0)
    mx=float(np.max(w_new))
    w_new=w_new-mx
    p=softmax_w(w_new)
    return p,w_new,m,v,t
```
If sd=0, w_new=w -0, recenter, softmax (same p maybe). State updated. Good.

    ...

```
def normalized_momentum(mom, d, beta):
   dclean = np.where(np.isfinite(d), d, 0.0)
   sd = np.sqrt(np.mean(dclean*dclean))
   if sd > 1e-15: dclean = dclean/sd
   mom = beta*mom + (1-beta)*dclean
   sd2=np.sqrt(np.mean(mom*mom))
   if sd2>1e-15: mom=mom/sd2
   return np.clip(mom, -5,5)
```
Then candidate `w_new = w - lr*mult*mom`.

`update_mom` top-level:
```
def update_momentum(mom, d, beta=0.9, clip=3.0):
    d=np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64)
    d=np.where(np.isfinite(d),d,0.0)
    sd=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(d*d)))
    if sd > 1e-15:
        d = d/sd
    mom = beta*mom + (1.0-beta)*d
    sd2=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(mom*mom)))
    if sd2 > 1e-15:
        mom = mom/sd2
    return np.clip(mom, -clip, clip)
```
Candidate step:
```
def step_from_w(w, mom, lr):
    w_new = w - lr*mom
    w_new=np.where(np.isfinite(w_new),w_new,0.0)
    w_new -= max
    p=softmax_w(w_new); return p,w_new
```

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    try: total_budget=float(budget_s)
    except: total_budget=2400.0
    total_budget=min(max(total_budget,10.0),2395.0)
    deadline=t0+total_budget
    rng_seed=int(seed) if seed is not None else 42
    parent=load_parent()
    base_n=4096
    parent_seq=None
    if parent is not None:
       if parent.size > MAX_N: parent = resample(parent, MAX_N)
       if parent.size < 16: parent = power_candidate(4096)
       parent_seq=normalize_nonneg(parent)
       base_n=parent_seq.size
    else:
       parent_seq=power_candidate(base_n)
    cands=[]
    if parent_seq is not None:
       cands.append(('parent', parent_seq.copy(), 0.50, rng_seed))
       long_n = 8192 if parent_seq.size < 8192 and parent_seq.size >= 2048 else max(4096,parent_seq.size)
       if long_n != parent_seq.size and long_n <= MAX_N:
          cands.append(('parent_long', resample_sequence(parent_seq,long_n), 0.22, rng_seed+1))
       # perturbed
       rngp=np.random.default_rng(rng_seed+2)
       pert=normalize_nonneg(softmax_w(log_weights(parent_seq)+rngp.normal(0,0.25,parent_seq.size)))
       cands.append(('parent_pert', pert, 0.14, rng_seed+2))
       # power
       cands.append(('power', power_candidate(parent_seq.size), 0.14, rng_seed+3))
    else:
       cands.append(('power', power_candidate(base_n),0.4,rng_seed))
       cands.append(('random', random_candidate(base_n,np.random.default_rng(rng_seed+1)),0.3,rng_seed+1))
       cands.append(('lowfreq', lowfreq_candidate(base_n,np.random.default_rng(rng_seed+2)),0.3,rng_seed+2))
    total_frac=sum(frac)
    global_top=[]
    for name,seq,frac,sg in cands:
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 10: break
       alloc=min(frac*total_budget, deadline-time.monotonic())
       if alloc < 25: continue
       print_info(f"Running {name} n={seq.size} alloc={alloc:.1f}s")
       out_seq, score = optimize_one(seq, seed=sg, budget_s=alloc, deadline=None)
       print_info(f"{name} finished score={score:.7f}")
       global_top=add_top(global_top,out_seq,score,MAX_TOP)
    # If no candidates, fallback
    if not global_top:
       return finalize_sequence(power_candidate(4096))
    # final exact validation optional
    best_seq=global_top[0][1]; best_score=global_top[0][0]
    # exact check top maybe
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 20:
       exact_best_seq=None; exact_score=INF; checked=0
       for sc,seq in global_top:
           if checked>=4 or time.monotonic() > deadline - 10: break
           n=seq.size
           # estimate exact time: t_est = (n/5000)^2 * 5?; skip if too big
           est = 5.0 * (max(1,n)/5000.0)**2
           if n > MAX_N or est > max(30.0, deadline-time.monotonic()-5):
               continue
           t1=time.monotonic()
           esc=exact_score_array(seq)
           dt=time.monotonic()-t1
           if np.isfinite(esc) and esc < exact_score:
               exact_score=esc; exact_best_seq=seq
           checked +=1
           if exact_best_seq is not None and (checked>=2 or exact_score < 1.505): break
       if exact_best_seq is not None and np.isfinite(exact_score):
           best_seq=exact_best_seq; best_score=exact_score
    return finalize_sequence(best_seq)
```

```
def sort_top_list(items):
    # returns new list sorted ascending by score
    lst=list(items)
    for i in range(len(lst)-1):
       m=i
       for j in range(i+1,len(lst)):
          if lst[j][0] < lst[m][0]: m=j
       lst[i],lst[m]=lst[m],lst[i]
    return lst
```

def optimize_one(a, seed=42, budget_s=2300.0):
    start=time.monotonic()

       outer+=1
       if not valid: reset

               if score > score_before +1e-10: reject? To avoid, compute full first? Candidate score_only uses same algorithm as full but only max; should match. We can use full score; if worse, don't accept.

Need `self_block` function top-level. It takes many args and returns (p,A,c,mx,score,w_f,mom_f,lr_self,improved). We'll implement.

```
def self_gradient_block(p,A,c,max_b,score,w_f,mom_f,lr_self,K,steps,deadline,N,rng,g_buf):
    improved=False
    for step in range(steps):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 0.05: break
        if c.size==0: break
        idx=choose_active_indices(c,K,rng)
        wts=make_weights_from_c(c,idx,1.2)
        h=weighted_shift_sum(p, idx,wts,g_buf)
        grad=2.0*h
        mean=float(np.dot(p,grad))
        d=p*(grad-mean)
        mom_f=update_momentum(mom_f,d)
        # try multipliers
        best_sc=score; best_p=None; best_w=None; best_mult=0
        for mult in (1.0,0.5,2.0):
            p_try,w_try=step_from_w(w_f,mom_f,lr_self*mult)
            sc_try=fft_score_only(p_try,N)
            if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc=sc_try; best_p=p_try; best_w=w_try; best_mult=mult
        if best_p is not None:
            A_new,c_new,mx_new,sc_full=fft_score_full(best_p,N)
            if np.isfinite(sc_full) and sc_full < score - 1e-12:
                p=best_p; w_f=best_w; mom_f=uupdated? already; A=A_new; c=c_new; max_b=mx_new; score=sc_full; improved=True; lr_self = ...
                # continue to next step
                if best_mult > 1.0: lr_self=min(0.25, lr_self*1.05)
                elif best_mult < 1.0: lr_self=max(0.02, lr_self*0.95)
            else:
                lr_self=max(0.02, lr_self*0.9)
                break
        else:
            lr_self=max(0.02, lr_self*0.85)
            break
    return p,A,c,max_b,score,w_f,mom_f,lr_self,improved
```
But `update_momentum` was called before candidate; if candidate accepted, mom_f already updated. If rejected, mom_f updated; we break. Good.

```
def try_blend_candidates(f,b,w_f,w_b,current_score,N, alphas=(0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0)):
   best_cand=None; best_sc=current_score
   for a in alphas:
      cc=(1-a)*f+a*b; s=cc.sum(); if s>0: cc /= s
      sc=fft_score_only(cc,N)
      if sc < best_sc - 1e-12: best_cand=cc; best_sc=sc
   # geometric 0.33,0.66 maybe
   for a in (0.33,0.66):
      wb=a*w_f+(1-a)*w_f? actually (1-a)*w_f+a*w_b
      gc=softmax_w((1-a)*w_f + a*w_b)
      sc=fft_score_only(gc,N)
      if sc < best_sc -1e-12: best_cand=gc; best_sc=sc
   return best_cand,best_sc
```

Now, load_parent:
```
def load_initial_sequence():
    obj=globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    if obj is None: return None
    try: arr=np.asarray(obj,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except: return None
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr),arr,0.0); arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
    s=arr.sum(); if s<=0: return None
    arr/=s
    if arr.size>MAX_N: arr=resample_sequence(arr,MAX_N)
    return arr
```

phase1=rng.uniform(0,2π)
w=0.7*np.cos(2π*i/n+phase1)+0.3*np.cos(4π*i/n+rng.uniform(0,2π))
return softmax_w(w - w.max())
```

`choose_active_indices`:
```
def choose_active_indices(c,K,rng,do_random=False):
   m=c.size
   if K<=0: return np.empty(0,int)
   if K>=m: return np.arange(m)
   pool=int(min(m, 2*K))
   if pool==m: pool_idx=np.arange(m)
   else: pool_idx=np.argpartition(c, -pool)[-pool:]
   if K>=pool.size: return pool_idx
   if do_random and rng is not None:
       return rng.choice(pool_idx, size=K, replace=False)
   # deterministic first K
   return pool_idx[:K]
```
Random selection from `pool_idx` unsorted; okay. If do_random False, `pool_idx` unsorted by c? argpartition returns partition, not sorted. First K are among top pool but not necessarily top K. That's okay but less sharp. We could sort `pool_idx` by c descending for deterministic. Sorting pool size <=400, cheap: `pool_idx = pool_idx[np.argsort(c[pool_idx])[::-1]]`. Do this for deterministic to get top K. For random, no need sort. We'll implement:
```
if do_random: ...
else:
   if pool.size>K:
      order=np.argsort(c[pool_idx])[::-1]
      pool_idx=pool_idx[order[:K]]
   return pool_idx
```

Now, `subproblem_block` detailed code:
```
def subproblem_block(f,F,b,w_b,mom_b,lr_b,K,steps,deadline,N,rng,g_buf):
    n=f.size; m=2*n-1
    for _ in range(steps):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 0.05: break
        B=np.fft.rfft(b,N)
        cross=np.fft.irfft(F*B,N)
        cross=cross[:m]
        cross=np.where(cross>0,cross,0.0)
        if cross.size==0: break
        mx=float(np.max(cross))
        if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx<=0: continue
        do_rand = bool(rng.random() < 0.3) if rng is not None else False
        idx=choose_active_indices(cross,K,rng,do_rand)
        if idx.size==0: continue
        wts=make_weights_from_c(cross,idx,1.0)
        h=weighted_shift_sum(f, idx, wts, g_buf)
        meanh=float(np.dot(b,h))
        d=b*(h-meanh)
        mom_b=update_momentum(mom_b,d)
        b_try,w_b_try=step_from_w(w_b,mom_b,lr_b)
        b=b_try; w_b=w_b_try
    # final mx
    B=np.fft.rfft(b,N); cross=np.fft.irfft(F*B,N); cross=cross[:m]; cross=np.maximum(cross,0.0)
    final=float(np.max(cross)) if cross.size else 0.0
    if not finite: final=INF
    return b,w_b,mom_b,final
```
We don't check if b improved; lr adapt in outer. If `b` diverges (many entries zero), softmax keeps positive.

`self_gradient_block` similar. We need pass `g_buf` separate to avoid conflict.

Actually cross coefficient sum_i f_{k-i} b_i ≤ max f * sum b? Since each coefficient sums subset of b, ≤max f *1. If f has peak can be 0.2, cross ≤0.2; no blow-up. b normalized sum1, f sum1, so cross values bounded by max f (could be 1 if p0=1). Fine.

Let's code `optimize_one` fully:

```
def optimize_one(a, seed=42, budget_s=2300.0):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    budget=max(1.0,float(budget_s)-2.0)
    deadline=t0+budget
    f=normalize_nonneg(a)
    n=f.size
    if n < 16:
       f=power_candidate(4096); n=f.size
    if n > MAX_N:
       f=resample_sequence(f, MAX_N); n=f.size
    N=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    A,c,max_b,score=fft_score_full(f,N)
    best=f.copy(); best_score=score
    w_f=log_weights(f); mom_f=np.zeros(n)
    b=f.copy(); w_b=log_weights(b); mom_b=np.zeros(n)
    lr_b=0.08; lr_self=0.05
    K=int(np.clip(0.010*(2*n-1),32,192))
    sub_steps=12 if n <= 8192 else 8
    self_steps=4 if n <= 8192 else 3
    stall=0; last_improve=t0
    sub_ema=score
    g_b=np.zeros(n); g_f=np.zeros(n)
    outer=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.05:
        outer += 1
        # validate
        if (not np.isfinite(score) or not np.all(np.isfinite(f)) or not np.all(np.isfinite(b))):
            f=best.copy(); score=best_score; A,c,max_b,score=fft_score_full(f,N)
            w_f=log_weights(f); mom_f=np.zeros(n)
            b=f.copy(); w_b=log_weights(b); mom_b=np.zeros(n)
            stall=0; continue
        # subproblem
        F=A
        b,w_b,mom_b,sub_mx = subproblem_block(f,F,b,w_b,mom_b,lr_b,K,sub_steps,deadline,N,rng,g_b)
        if np.isfinite(sub_mx):
            sub_ema = 0.75*sub_ema + 0.25*sub_mx
            if sub_ema > score * 1.05:
                lr_b = max(0.02, lr_b*0.9)
            elif sub_ema < score * 0.97:
                lr_b = min(0.25, lr_b*1.03)
        improved=False
        # blend
        cand, cand_score = try_blend_candidates(f,b,w_f,w_b,score,N)
        if cand is not None and cand_score < score - 1e-12:
            A2,c2,mx2,full_sc=fft_score_full(cand,N)
            if np.isfinite(full_sc) and full_sc < score - 1e-12:
                f=cand.copy(); score=full_sc; A=A2; c=c2; max_b=mx2
                w_f=log_weights(f); mom_f=np.zeros(n)
                if float(np.max(np.abs(f-b))) < 1e-8:
                    b=f.copy(); w_b=log_weights(b); mom_b=np.zeros(n)
                improved=True
        if not improved and time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.3:
            f,A,c,max_b,score,w_f,mom_f,lr_self,improved = self_gradient_block(
                f,A,c,max_b,score,w_f,mom_f,lr_self,K,self_steps,deadline,N,rng,g_f)
        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score=score; best=f.copy(); last_improve=time.monotonic(); stall=0
            lr_self=min(0.2, lr_self*1.04); lr_b=min(0.25, lr_b*1.02)
        else:
            stall += 1
            lr_self=max(0.02, lr_self*0.985); lr_b=max(0.03, lr_b*0.995)
        if stall > 80:
            # K adjust
            if K < 256:
                K = int(min(256, K + 8))
            elif K > 64:
                K = int(max(64, K - 8))
        if stall > 150 or time.monotonic() - last_improve > 180.0:
            # perturb
            explored=False
            for sig in (0.05,0.12,0.25,0.5):
                w_try=log_weights(best) + rng.normal(0.0,sig,size=n)
                pt=softmax_w(w_try)
                st=fft_score_only(pt,N)
                if np.isfinite(st) and st < score + 0.02:
                    f=pt; score=st; A,c,max_b,score=fft_score_full(f,N); explored=True; break
            if not explored:
                f=best.copy(); score=best_score; A,c,max_b,score=fft_score_full(f,N)
            w_f=log_weights(f); mom_f=np.zeros(n)
            b=f.copy(); w_b=log_weights(b); mom_b=np.zeros(n)
            stall=0
            # slight boost lr
            lr_self=max(0.03, lr_self*1.1); lr_b=max(0.05, lr_b*1.1)
        if outer % 80 == 0:
            print_info(f"outer={outer} n={n} score={score:.7f} best={best_score:.7f} lr_self={lr_self:.4f} lr_b={lr_b:.4f} K={K}")
    # final recompute exact? return best
    return best,best_score
```

```
def try_blend_candidates(f,b,w_f,w_b,current_score,N):
    best_cand=None; best_sc=current_score - 1e-12? Set to current_score
    alphas=(0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0)
    for alpha in alphas:
       cc=(1-alpha)*f+alpha*b
       s=float(cc.sum())
       if s <=0: continue
       cc=cc/s
       sc=fft_score_only(cc,N)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
           best_cand=cc; best_sc=sc
    # geometric
    for alpha in (0.33,0.66):
       wbl=(1-alpha)*w_f + alpha*w_b
       gc=softmax_w(wbl)
       sc=fft_score_only(gc,N)
       if ...:
           best_cand=gc; best_sc=sc
    return best_cand,best_sc
```
Since `softmax_w` recenters w, okay.

`resample_sequence`: If target_n <=0 return ones. Use np.interp. Ensure normalize.

```
def resample_sequence(a, target_n):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a); n=a.size
    if n==target_n: return a
    if target_n <=1: return np.ones(target_n) if target_n>0 else np.ones(1)
    x0=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    x1=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,target_n)
    b=np.interp(x1, x0, a)
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    s=b.sum()
    if s<=0: b=np.ones(target_n)
    else: b=b/s
    return b
```
Downsampling by interpolation may lose endpoint masses? Interp at endpoints includes first/last values. Good.

`power_candidate` maybe improve by using `idx = np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float); p = 1/np.sqrt(idx) + 1e-6`? The endpoint p0=1. Good. Normalize.

`random_candidate`: If using `softmax_w(rng.normal)`, sequence may be random smooth? Normal over indices creates front/back bias. Could be okay. Add sign? Maybe use `w = rng.normal(0,1,n); p=softmax_w(w - 0.5*w.mean?)` no. Better lowfreq random smoother:
```
w=0.0
for freq in (1,2,4,8): amp=0.5/freq; w += amp*np.cos(2np.pi*freq*i/n + rng.uniform(0,2π))
p=softmax_w(w-w.max())
```
This avoids high-frequency noise. We'll use for random.

```
def smooth_random_candidate(n,rng):
   i=np.arange(n)
   w=np.zeros(n)
   for frac,amp in zip((1,2,4,8),(0.8,0.4,0.2,0.1)):
       w += amp*np.cos((2.0*np.pi*frac*i)/float(n) + rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*np.pi))
   return softmax_w(w)
```
Good.

Now, possible issue with using `softmax_w` for random w: If w all small, p near uniform; if w amplitude up to1.5, sequence biased. Good.

Now, code for `exact_score_array` robust:
```
def exact_score_array(a):
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=arr.size
    if n==0: return INF
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr),arr,0.0)
    arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
    s=float(arr.sum())
    if s < 0.01: return INF
    b=np.convolve(arr,arr)
    if b.size==0: return INF
    mx=float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(mx): return INF
    val=float(2.0*n*mx/(s*s))
    return val if np.isfinite(val) else INF
```
If arr has length huge, memory for b length 2n-1 ~16k, fine. Computation heavy.

```
def add_top(top, seq, score, max_items=MAX_TOP):
    if not np.isfinite(score): return top
    seq=np.asarray(seq,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if len(top)<max_items: top.append((score,seq)); return top
    worst=0
    for i in range(1,len(top)):
       if top[i][0] > top[worst][0]: worst=i
    if score < top[worst][0]: top[worst]=(score,seq)
    return top
```

def full_fft_self_step(p,A,c,max_b,score,w_f,mom_f,lr,N, smooth_K=256):
   if max_b <=0: return ...
   tau=max_b/max(1,smooth_K)
   lam=softmax_values(c,tau)
   L=np.fft.rfft(lam,N)
   h=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*L,N)[:p.size]
   grad=2.0*h
   mean=float(np.dot(p,grad))
   d=p*(grad-mean)
   mom_f=update_momentum(mom_f,d)
   best_sc=score; best_p=None; best_w=None; best_mult=0
   for mult in (1.0,0.5,2.0):
      p_try,w_try=step_from_w(w_f,mom_f,lr*mult)
      sc=fft_score_only(p_try,N)
      if sc < best_sc -1e-12: ...
   if best_p is not None:
       A2,c2,mx2,sc_full=fft_score_full(best_p,N)
       if sc_full < score -1e-12:
          return best_p,A2,c2,mx2,sc_full,w_try,mom_f,lr* (adapt), True
   return p,A,c,max_b,score,w_f,mom_f,lr*0.9,False

```python
"""
Search for non-negative coefficient sequences minimizing
    2 * n * max(np.convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)**2.

The optimizer works on the scale-invariant simplex sum(a)=1, uses FFT-only
objective evaluations, and alternates between
  1. a convex "cross-convolution" subproblem update for fixed f:
       minimize max_k (f * b)_k over b >= 0, sum b = 1,
  2. natural-gradient / active-set self-convolution descent,
  3. geometric and Euclidean blending with the cross-subproblem point,
  4. occasional full-spectrum smoothed self-gradient steps,
  5. random/log-space perturbations and restarts.

The code keeps the best candidate, validates a few candidates with
exact numpy.convolve near the end when time permits, and returns a valid
non-finite/non-negative float list.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

EPS = 1e-300
INF = float("inf")
MAX_N = 8192
MAX_TOP = 10


def print_info(msg):
    print(msg, flush=True)


def normalize_nonneg(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        arr = np.ones(max(1, arr.size), dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(arr.sum())
    return arr / s


def softmax_w(w):
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if w.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(w))
    e = np.exp(w - mx)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(w.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(w.size)
    return e / s


def log_weights(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    w = np.log(p)
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    w -= float(np.max(w))
    return w


def next_fast_len(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    p = 1
    while p < int(m):
        p <<= 1
    return p


def fft_score_full(a, N):
    n = len(a)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)
    c = c[:m]
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    if c.size:
        mx = float(np.max(c))
    else:
        mx = 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return A, c, mx, INF
    sc = float(2.0 * n * mx)
    return A, c, mx, sc


def fft_score_only(a, N):
    n = len(a)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)
    c = c[:m]
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    if c.size:
        mx = float(np.max(c))
    else:
        mx = 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return INF
    return float(2.0 * n * mx)


def softmax_values(x, temperature):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(temperature) or temperature <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(max(1, x.size)) / max(1, x.size)
    y = x / float(temperature)
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(y))
    y = y - mx
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(x.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(x.size)
    return e / s


def choose_active_indices(c, K, rng, do_random=False):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    m = c.size
    if K <= 0 or m == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.intp)
    if K >= m:
        return np.arange(m, dtype=np.intp)

    pool = int(min(m, 2 * int(K)))
    if pool <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.intp)

    if pool >= m:
        pool_idx = np.arange(m, dtype=np.intp)
    else:
        pool_idx = np.argpartition(c, -pool)[-pool:].astype(np.intp, copy=False)

    if K >= pool_idx.size:
        return pool_idx

    if do_random and rng is not None:
        return rng.choice(pool_idx, size=int(K), replace=False)

    order = np.argsort(c[pool_idx])[::-1]
    return pool_idx[order[:int(K)]].astype(np.intp, copy=False)


def make_weights_from_c(c, idx, sharp=1.0):
    idx = np.asarray(idx, dtype=np.intp).ravel()
    if idx.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    vals = np.asarray(c[idx], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    vals = np.where(np.isfinite(vals), vals, 0.0)
    vals = np.maximum(vals, 0.0)
    if sharp != 1.0:
        vals = np.power(vals + 1e-300, float(sharp))
    s = float(vals.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(vals.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(vals.size)
    return vals / s


def weighted_shift_sum(seq, k_arr, w_arr, out=None):
    # Computes h_i = sum_k w_k seq[k-i] for valid k-i in [0, n-1].
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = seq.size
    if out is None:
        g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        g = np.asarray(out, dtype=np.float64)
        g.fill(0.0)

    n = int(n)
    for kk, wt in zip(np.asarray(k_arr, dtype=np.intp).ravel(),
                      np.asarray(w_arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()):
        if wt == 0.0:
            continue
        k = int(kk)
        if k < 0 or k >= 2 * n - 1:
            continue
        if k >= n - 1:
            i0 = 0
        else:
            i0 = k - (n - 1)
        i1 = n - 1 if k >= n - 1 else k
        if i0 > i1:
            continue
        j0 = k - i1
        j1 = k - i0
        g[i0:i1 + 1] += wt * seq[j0:j1 + 1][::-1]
    return g


def update_momentum(mom, d, beta=0.9, clip=3.0):
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    d = np.where(np.isfinite(d), d, 0.0)
    sd = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(d * d)))
    if sd > 1e-15:
        d = d / sd
    mom = beta * mom + (1.0 - beta) * d
    sd2 = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(mom * mom)))
    if sd2 > 1e-15:
        mom = mom / sd2
    return np.clip(mom, -clip, clip)


def step_from_w(w, mom, lr):
    w_new = w - float(lr) * mom
    w_new = np.where(np.isfinite(w_new), w_new, 0.0)
    w_new -= float(np.max(w_new))
    p = softmax_w(w_new)
    return p, w_new


def exact_score_array(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = arr.size
    if n == 0:
        return INF
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return INF
    b = np.convolve(arr, arr)
    if b.size == 0:
        return INF
    mx = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return INF
    val = float(2.0 * n * mx / (s * s))
    return val if np.isfinite(val) else INF


def load_initial_sequence():
    obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if obj is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(obj, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size < 8:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    arr = arr / s
    if arr.size > MAX_N:
        arr = resample_sequence(arr, MAX_N)
    return arr


def resample_sequence(a, target_n):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    target_n = int(target_n)
    if target_n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == target_n:
        return a
    if target_n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, target_n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.interp(x1, x0, a)
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        b = np.ones(target_n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        b = b / s
    return b


def power_candidate(n):
    n = int(max(16, n))
    x = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    p = 1.0 / np.sqrt(x)
    p += 1e-12
    return normalize_nonneg(p)


def smooth_random_candidate(n, rng):
    n = int(max(16, n))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for freq, amp in ((1, 0.9), (2, 0.45), (4, 0.25), (8, 0.12)):
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        w += amp * np.cos((2.0 * np.pi * freq * i) / float(n) + ph)
    return softmax_w(w)


def add_top(top, seq, score, max_items=MAX_TOP):
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return top
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if seq.size == 0:
        return top
    if len(top) < int(max_items):
        top.append((float(score), seq))
        return top
    worst = 0
    for i in range(1, len(top)):
        if top[i][0] > top[worst][0]:
            worst = i
    if float(score) < float(top[worst][0]):
        top[worst] = (float(score), seq)
    return top


def sort_top_list(items):
    # Stable enough selection sort; avoids lambda usage.
    lst = list(items)
    for i in range(len(lst) - 1):
        m = i
        for j in range(i + 1, len(lst)):
            if lst[j][0] < lst[m][0]:
                m = j
        if m != i:
            lst[i], lst[m] = lst[m], lst[i]
    return lst


def subproblem_block(f, F, b, w_b, mom_b, lr_b, K, steps, deadline, N, rng, g_buf):
    n = f.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    for _ in range(int(steps)):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 0.05:
            break
        B = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
        cross = np.fft.irfft(F * B, N)
        cross = cross[:m]
        cross = np.where(cross > 0.0, cross, 0.0)
        if cross.size == 0:
            break
        mx = float(np.max(cross))
        if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0.0:
            continue
        do_rand = bool(rng.random() < 0.35) if rng is not None else False
        idx = choose_active_indices(cross, K, rng, do_rand)
        if idx.size == 0:
            continue
        wts = make_weights_from_c(cross, idx, 1.0)
        h = weighted_shift_sum(f, idx, wts, g_buf)
        meanh = float(np.dot(b, h))
        d = b * (h - meanh)
        mom_b = update_momentum(mom_b, d)
        b_new, w_b = step_from_w(w_b, mom_b, lr_b)
        b = b_new

    B = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
    cross = np.fft.irfft(F * B, N)
    cross = cross[:m]
    cross = np.where(cross > 0.0, cross, 0.0)
    final_mx = float(np.max(cross)) if cross.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(final_mx):
        final_mx = INF
    return b, w_b, mom_b, final_mx


def self_gradient_block(p, A, c, max_b, score, w_f, mom_f, lr_self, K, steps, deadline, N, rng, g_buf):
    n = p.size
    improved = False
    for _ in range(int(steps)):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 0.05:
            break
        if c.size == 0 or max_b <= 0.0:
            break
        do_rand = bool(rng.random() < 0.35) if rng is not None else False
        idx = choose_active_indices(c, K, rng, do_rand)
        if idx.size == 0:
            break
        wts = make_weights_from_c(c, idx, 1.2)
        h = weighted_shift_sum(p, idx, wts, g_buf)
        grad = 2.0 * h
        mean_grad = float(np.dot(p, grad))
        d = p * (grad - mean_grad)
        mom_f = update_momentum(mom_f, d)

        best_sc = float(score)
        best_p = None
        best_w = None
        best_mult = 0.0
        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 2.0):
            p_try, w_try = step_from_w(w_f, mom_f, lr_self * mult)
            sc_try = fft_score_only(p_try, N)
            if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc = sc_try
                best_p = p_try
                best_w = w_try
                best_mult = mult

        if best_p is not None:
            A2, c2, mx2, full_sc = fft_score_full(best_p, N)
            if np.isfinite(full_sc) and full_sc < float(score) - 1e-12:
                p = best_p
                A = A2
                c = c2
                max_b = mx2
                score = full_sc
                w_f = best_w
                improved = True
                if best_mult > 1.0:
                    lr_self = min(0.25, lr_self * 1.05)
                elif best_mult < 1.0:
                    lr_self = max(0.02, lr_self * 0.96)
                continue

        lr_self = max(0.02, lr_self * 0.85)
        break
    return p, A, c, max_b, score, w_f, mom_f, lr_self, improved


def full_fft_self_step(p, A, c, max_b, score, w_f, mom_f, lr_self, N, smooth_K=256):
    if c.size == 0 or max_b <= 0.0:
        return p, A, c, max_b, score, w_f, mom_f, lr_self, False
    n = p.size
    tau = max_b / float(max(1, int(smooth_K)))
    lam = softmax_values(c, tau)
    L = np.fft.rfft(lam, N)
    h = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A) * L, N)
    h = h[:n]
    grad = 2.0 * h
    mean_grad = float(np.dot(p, grad))
    d = p * (grad - mean_grad)
    mom_f = update_momentum(mom_f, d)

    best_sc = float(score)
    best_p = None
    best_w = None
    best_mult = 0.0
    for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 2.0):
        p_try, w_try = step_from_w(w_f, mom_f, lr_self * mult)
        sc_try = fft_score_only(p_try, N)
        if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = sc_try
            best_p = p_try
            best_w = w_try
            best_mult = mult

    if best_p is not None:
        A2, c2, mx2, full_sc = fft_score_full(best_p, N)
        if np.isfinite(full_sc) and full_sc < float(score) - 1e-12:
            if best_mult > 1.0:
                lr_self = min(0.25, lr_self * 1.05)
            elif best_mult < 1.0:
                lr_self = max(0.02, lr_self * 0.96)
            return best_p, A2, c2, mx2, full_sc, best_w, mom_f, lr_self, True

    lr_self = max(0.02, lr_self * 0.92)
    return p, A, c, max_b, score, w_f, mom_f, lr_self, False


def try_blend_candidates(f, b, w_f, w_b, current_score, N):
    best_cand = None
    best_sc = float(current_score)

    for alpha in (0.10, 0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 1.00):
        cc = (1.0 - alpha) * f + alpha * b
        s = float(cc.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            continue
        cc = cc / s
        sc = fft_score_only(cc, N)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_cand = cc
            best_sc = sc

    for alpha in (0.33, 0.66):
        wbl = (1.0 - alpha) * w_f + alpha * w_b
        gc = softmax_w(wbl)
        sc = fft_score_only(gc, N)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_cand = gc
            best_sc = sc

    return best_cand, best_sc


def optimize_one(a, seed=42, budget_s=2300.0):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 2.0)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2200.0
    deadline = t0 + budget

    f = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = f.size
    if n < 16:
        f = power_candidate(4096)
        n = f.size
    if n > MAX_N:
        f = resample_sequence(f, MAX_N)
        n = f.size

    N = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    A, c, max_b, score = fft_score_full(f, N)
    best = f.copy()
    best_score = float(score)
    local_top = add_top([], best, best_score, 6)

    w_f = log_weights(f)
    mom_f = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = f.copy()
    w_b = log_weights(b)
    mom_b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    lr_b = 0.08
    lr_self = 0.05
    K = int(np.clip(0.010 * (2 * n - 1), 32, 192))
    sub_steps = 12 if n <= 8192 else 8
    self_steps = 4 if n <= 8192 else 3

    stall = 0
    last_improve = t0
    sub_ema = best_score
    g_b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    g_f = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    outer = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.05:
        outer += 1

        if (not np.isfinite(score) or not np.all(np.isfinite(f)) or not np.all(np.isfinite(b))):
            f = best.copy()
            score = best_score
            A, c, max_b, score = fft_score_full(f, N)
            w_f = log_weights(f)
            mom_f = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            b = f.copy()
            w_b = log_weights(b)
            mom_b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            stall = 0
            continue

        F = A
        b, w_b, mom_b, sub_mx = subproblem_block(
            f, F, b, w_b, mom_b, lr_b, K, sub_steps, deadline, N, rng, g_b
        )

        if np.isfinite(sub_mx):
            sub_ema = 0.75 * sub_ema + 0.25 * sub_mx
            if sub_ema > score * 1.05:
                lr_b = max(0.02, lr_b * 0.9)
            elif sub_ema < score * 0.97:
                lr_b = min(0.25, lr_b * 1.03)

        improved = False

        if (np.isfinite(sub_mx) and sub_mx < score * 0.999) or (stall > 20 and outer % 2 == 0):
            cand, cand_score = try_blend_candidates(f, b, w_f, w_b, score, N)
            if cand is not None and cand_score < score - 1e-12:
                A2, c2, mx2, full_sc = fft_score_full(cand, N)
                if np.isfinite(full_sc) and full_sc < score - 1e-12:
                    f = cand.copy()
                    score = full_sc
                    A = A2
                    c = c2
                    max_b = mx2
                    w_f = log_weights(f)
                    mom_f = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    if float(np.max(np.abs(f - b))) < 1e-8:
                        b = f.copy()
                        w_b = log_weights(b)
                        mom_b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    improved = True

        if not improved and time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.3:
            f, A, c, max_b, score, w_f, mom_f, lr_self, improved_self = self_gradient_block(
                f, A, c, max_b, score, w_f, mom_f, lr_self, K, self_steps, deadline, N, rng, g_f
            )
            improved = improved_self or improved

        if (not improved and outer % 40 == 0 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0):
            f, A, c, max_b, score, w_f, mom_f, lr_self, improved_full = full_fft_self_step(
                f, A, c, max_b, score, w_f, mom_f, lr_self, N, 256
            )
            improved = improved or improved_full

        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = float(score)
            best = f.copy()
            local_top = add_top(local_top, best, best_score, 6)
            last_improve = time.monotonic()
            stall = 0
            lr_self = min(0.20, lr_self * 1.04)
            lr_b = min(0.25, lr_b * 1.02)
        else:
            stall += 1
            lr_self = max(0.02, lr_self * 0.985)
            lr_b = max(0.03, lr_b * 0.995)

        if stall > 80:
            if K < 256:
                K = int(min(256, K + 8))
            elif K > 64:
                K = int(max(64, K - 8))

        if stall > 150 or time.monotonic() - last_improve > 180.0:
            base_seq = best
            if local_top and rng.random() < 0.45:
                base_seq = local_top[int(rng.integers(0, len(local_top)))][1]

            explored = False
            for sig in (0.05, 0.12, 0.25, 0.50):
                w_try = log_weights(base_seq) + rng.normal(0.0, sig, size=n)
                pt = softmax_w(w_try)
                st = fft_score_only(pt, N)
                if np.isfinite(st) and st < score + 0.02:
                    f = pt
                    score = st
                    A, c, max_b, score = fft_score_full(f, N)
                    explored = True
                    break

            if not explored:
                f = best.copy()
                score = best_score
                A, c, max_b, score = fft_score_full(f, N)

            w_f = log_weights(f)
            mom_f = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            b = f.copy()
            w_b = log_weights(b)
            mom_b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            stall = 0
            last_improve = time.monotonic()
            lr_self = max(0.03, lr_self * 1.10)
            lr_b = max(0.05, lr_b * 1.10)

        if outer % 80 == 0:
            print_info(
                "outer=%d n=%d score=%.7f best=%.7f lr_self=%.4f lr_b=%.4f K=%d"
                % (outer, n, score, best_score, lr_self, lr_b, K)
            )

    if np.isfinite(best_score):
        return best, float(best_score)
    return f, float(score)


def finalize_sequence(a):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    scale = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n))
    a = a * scale
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s2 = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s2) or s2 < 0.01:
        a = a * (10.0 / max(s2, 1e-300))
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return [float(x) for x in a]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total_budget = min(max(float(budget_s), 10.0), 2395.0)
    except Exception:
        total_budget = 2395.0
    deadline = t0 + total_budget
    base_seed = int(seed) if seed is not None else 42

    parent = load_initial_sequence()
    has_parent = parent is not None and parent.size >= 16
    if not has_parent:
        parent = power_candidate(4096)

    cands = []
    if has_parent:
        cands.append(("parent", parent.copy(), 0.42, base_seed))
        long_n = 8192 if parent.size < 8192 and parent.size >= 2048 else None
        if long_n is not None and long_n != parent.size:
            cands.append(("parent_long", resample_sequence(parent, long_n), 0.20, base_seed + 1))
        rng_p = np.random.default_rng(base_seed + 2)
        pert = softmax_w(log_weights(parent) + rng_p.normal(0.0, 0.25, size=parent.size))
        cands.append(("parent_pert", normalize_nonneg(pert), 0.12, base_seed + 2))
        cands.append(("power", power_candidate(parent.size), 0.12, base_seed + 3))
    else:
        cands.append(("power", power_candidate(4096), 0.35, base_seed))
        cands.append(("random", smooth_random_candidate(4096, np.random.default_rng(base_seed + 1)), 0.30, base_seed + 1))
        cands.append(("lowfreq", smooth_random_candidate(4096, np.random.default_rng(base_seed + 2)), 0.20, base_seed + 2))

    global_top = []

    for name, seq, frac, sg in cands:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 10.0:
            break
        allocated = min(float(frac) * total_budget, deadline - time.monotonic())
        if allocated < 25.0:
            continue
        print_info("Running candidate %s n=%d allocated=%.1fs" % (name, seq.size, allocated))
        out_seq, out_score = optimize_one(seq, seed=sg, budget_s=allocated)
        print_info("Candidate %s finished fft_score=%.7f" % (name, out_score))
        global_top = add_top(global_top, out_seq, out_score, MAX_TOP)

    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 30.0 and global_top:
        st = sort_top_list(global_top)
        ext_seq = st[0][1]
        print_info("Continuing best sequence n=%d with remaining %.1fs" % (ext_seq.size, remaining - 12.0))
        out_seq, out_score = optimize_one(ext_seq, seed=base_seed + 1000, budget_s=max(20.0, remaining - 12.0))
        print_info("Continuation finished fft_score=%.7f" % out_score)
        global_top = add_top(global_top, out_seq, out_score, MAX_TOP)

    if not global_top:
        return finalize_sequence(power_candidate(4096))

    sorted_top = sort_top_list(global_top)
    best_seq = sorted_top[0][1]
    best_score = sorted_top[0][0]

    # Light exact validation of a few top FFT candidates, if time permits.
    exact_best_seq = None
    exact_best_score = INF
    checked = 0
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 8.0:
        for sc_fft, seq in sorted_top:
            if checked >= 3:
                break
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            n = seq.size
            if n <= 0 or n > MAX_N:
                continue
            est = 10.0 * (float(max(1, n)) / 5000.0) ** 2
            if est > rem - 10.0:
                continue
            t1 = time.monotonic()
            esc = exact_score_array(seq)
            dt = time.monotonic() - t1
            checked += 1
            if np.isfinite(esc):
                print_info("Exact check n=%d score=%.9f (fft=%.9f) time=%.2fs" % (n, esc, sc_fft, dt))
                if esc < exact_best_score:
                    exact_best_score = esc
                    exact_best_seq = seq
            if exact_best_seq is not None and (exact_best_score < 1.500 or checked >= 3 or time.monotonic() > deadline - 12.0):
                break

    if exact_best_seq is not None and np.isfinite(exact_best_score):
        best_seq = exact_best_seq
        print_info("Final selected exact_score=%.9f" % exact_best_score)
    else:
        print_info("Final selected fft_score=%.9f" % best_score)

    return finalize_sequence(best_seq)
```
